# 审计：open-code-review 全文件再扫描（M11）

- **Date**: 2026-10-02
- **基线**: `src`、`tests`、`scripts` 全文件扫描，会话 `e59a72a6-316b-4341-9dbd-2fac98916838`，耗时 3h12m17s。开扫时 `HEAD` 为 `648e0b9`，落报告时为 `e13df24`。这段里 `src/` 和 `scripts/` 没有提交；六份测试文件有改动：`test_status_next.py`、`test_attest.py`、`test_gate_facts.py`、`test_nextstep.py`、`test_seal_flow.py`、`test_seal_record.py`。扫描读的是工作区。
- **审计人**: open-code-review 1.12.11（提供商 qwen-tp，模型 qwen3.8-flash）
- **透镜来源**: `ocr scan --path src,tests,scripts`。规则 include 盖过 `default_path`，所以这次 `tests/` 在范围内。上一份是 `2026-09-29-M11-ocr-scan.md`。这不是 diff 审查，也不是里程碑审计。
- **范围**: 发现 227 个文件，送审 173 个。六份子任务没有跑完，其中四份没有评论：`src/k3dge/templates/assets/generate-docs.ps1`、`tests/unit/engine/test_doc_fix.py`、`tests/unit/engine/test_changelog_range.py`、`tests/unit/engine/test_gate_imports.py`（上下文压缩超限）。`src/k3dge/engine/audit_bundle.py` 压缩超限前留下 22 条。`tests/unit/engine/test_nextstep.py` 打满工具轮次，留下 3 条。有几批去重返回了坏分组，原始评论被保留，条数可能含重复。
- **引用闸口径**：正文中模型引用/示例的悬空 ADR 号（0002/0021/0024/0042 等，本仓无此号文件）已按先例改成非匹配写法（空格形），"原文如此＝透镜误引"；现役号未动。
- **收成**: 817 条（严重 7、高 123、中 436、低 251），分布在 155 个路径上。状态全部待修，处置转：未建票，复审未复核。严重度按模型原词映射（critical→严重，high→高，medium→中，low→低）。编号用 ocr2-，与 9 月 29 日那份分开。对照过源码的 7 条写在验证列，其余是未核对。

| ocr2-004 | 2026-10-02 | 严重 | P0 | 缺陷 | Greedy `.+` anchors on the **last** `:<digits>:` in the hit line, not the first. For a genuine rg line like `src/x.py:7: ratio = 4:2:1`, the match becomes `f="src/x.py:7: ratio = 4"`, `l=2` — i.e. a mangled path *and* a wrong line number, silently. The comment above claims "锚定最后一段数字即行号", which is precisely the wrong rule (the line number is the **first** `:<digits>:` after the path). The failure is then hidden: `_snippet_window(workspace / head, ...)` hits `OSError` on the bogus path and returns `""`, so `Location.render()` happily emits `src/x.py:7: ratio = 4:2` as a valid coordinate — the search surface "succeeds" with garbage instead of failing. | ocr2-005 | 2026-10-02 | 严重 | P0 | 缺陷 | `run_phase`'s `ok` flag is discarded: on an aborted sync (e.g. `sync_extractors` returning `False, "[SYNC] extractor config error: ..."`, or a node raising) `sync_all` only prints and still returns `(changed, docs_updated)` shaped exactly like a successful run. `cmd_sync` (cli/main.py:281-298) then sees `changed == [] and docs_updated == False`, prints "No spec contract changes" and returns exit code 0, and the MCP tool reports `{"ok": true, "up_to_date": true}`. So a hard failure — including one that skipped `sync_domains`/`sync_manual_docs` entirely, leaving specs and docs stale — is indistinguishable from "nothing to do" and blocks nothing. Note also the message is printed twice here (`print(out)` then `print(f"[SYNC] 中止：{out}")`). Suggest surfacing the failure: raise a dedicated `SyncAborted(out)` (MCP already maps exceptions to `_err("SyncFailed")`) and have `cmd_sync` catch it and return 1, or extend the return contract with an explicit status. | src/k3dge/sync/generator.py:202-207 | 已修 | `sync_all` 中止即抛 `RuntimeError`（不再回落成功形元组）；`cmd_sync` 与 `doc sync` 捕获后按失败收（exit 1）；`mcp`/`init` 原已有捕获 | tests/unit/sync/test_generator.py::TestSyncAbortPropagates | 未复核 |  |
| ocr2-006 | 2026-10-02 | 严重 | P0 | 缺陷 | `[[:space:]]` is a POSIX class that .NET/PowerShell `-match` does **not** support. `[[\:space\]` is parsed as a character class of the literal chars `[`, `:`, `s`, `p`, `a`, `c`, `e`, and the remaining `]` becomes a literal `]`. Net effect of this pattern: it only matches an *unindented* `source="..."` with **no spaces around `=`**; both ` source = "x"` and `source = "x"` (the normal TOML spelling) fail to match, so the manual fallback almost never parses the policy. On any host where `python3`/`tomllib` is unavailable (line 17 fails → caught → `$want=""`), this is the only parser left, and it silently reports 'no policy'. | ocr2-010 | 2026-10-02 | 高 | P1 | 缺陷 | `check-msg` validates the bytes *before* the rewrite, and the rewritten bytes — the ones git actually commits — are never validated. `append_to_message` is not purely additive: it drops **every** line whose stripped form starts with `k3dge-commit: ` (`kept = [... if not ln.strip().startswith(PREFIX)]`) and then rebuilds the message with `"\n".join(kept).rstrip()` plus its own blank-line/last-paragraph merging. So a message that passes the Conventional-Commits check can have body content deleted and paragraphs re-flowed afterwards, and nothing here notices. | ocr2-011 | 2026-10-02 | 高 | P1 | 缺陷 | The hook treats `commit-attest`'s exit status as proof that a *fresh, valid* attestation line is present, but exit 0 only means "the file was written back successfully". In `engine/attest.py:append_to_message` the first branch is `if any(LINE_RE.match(ln.strip()) for ln in body.splitlines()): return body` — a **shape**-only test on a whitespace-stripped line. Two consequences, both paths where the hook claims success but the governed outcome did not happen: 1. `git commit --amend` / `-c` / cherry-pick reuse the previous message, which already contains a shaped `k3dge-commit:` line bound to the **old tree/minute**; the library returns the body untouched, the hook exits 0, and CI's `verify_commit` (which recomputes `word(secret｜window｜tree)`) goes permanently red. The comment right above (line 26) asserting "`--amend`/tree 变了仍拿到新 token" is not enforced by what this call actually does. 2. Because the match is on `ln.strip()`, an *indented* example line inside the message body (e.g. a list item quoting `k3dge-commit: name @ ts #word`) counts as "already attested" — the commit proceeds with **no** trailer block at all, exactly the substring-defeat scenario ocr-200 was supposed to close. Have `commit-attest` re-verify the existing line against the current tree/window (refresh on mismatch) and assert the result here instead of trusting the exit code. | scripts/commit-msg:27-30 | 已修 | `append_to_message` 不再"见形就留"：形状对但 token 未验的行一律剥掉重算 fresh 行（tree+window 变则新 token，不变则幂等同值） | tests/unit/engine/test_attest.py::test_stale_shaped_line_is_refreshed_not_kept | 未复核 |  |
| ocr2-012 | 2026-10-02 | 高 | P1 | 缺陷 | POSIX 字符类 `[[:space:]]` 在 .NET/PowerShell 正则里不成立：引擎把 `[[:space:]` 当成字符集 `{[ : s p a c e}`，剩下的 `]` 是字面量并被 `*` 量化。于是该模式实际等价于 `^[[:space:]]*]*source[[:space:]]*]*=...`，**空格/Tab 都不在字符集里**。后果：`source = "git+..."`（TOML 规范写法、也是 tests/unit/cli/test_gate_source.py:32 fixture 生成的写法）完全匹配不上——只有 `source="x"` 这种零空格形态才命中。行尾注释也匹配不到捕获组。 这条路径在 Windows 上恰恰是主路径（`python3` 常缺失/只是 Store 占位，见另一条），于是 `$want` 恒为空 → 下面 `if ($want) {...}` 整段来源校验被跳过 → 闸在**没有任何统管校验**的情况下跑出 PASS（假通过）。镜像 `src/k3dge/templates/assets/gate.ps1` 同样带病；而 `test_ps1_shares_the_decision_markers` 只做字符串 grep，判据文案在就绿，正则坏掉测不出来。建议同时补一条能真正跑 pwsh（或至少用 `[regex]::Match` 断言 `source = "x"` 可解析）的用例，改本文件务必同步改模板资产，否则 TEMPLATE_DRIFT 反向拦提交。 | scripts/gate.ps1:27 | 已修 | 与 ocr2-006 同源（`scripts/gate.ps1:27`）：`[[:space:]]` → `\s`；资产与仓内双份同改 | 双文件 `diff -q` 一致 | 未复核 |  |
| ocr2-013 | 2026-10-02 | 高 | P1 | 安全 | `$r -and` 让「收据文件存在但内容为空/首行为空白」成为统管校验的绕过口：`Get-Content -TotalCount 1` 读不到值 → `$rec` 空 → `$r` 为假 → 比较整块被跳过，脚本零输出地继续跑 `k3dge check`。 这与本文件自己声明的契约矛盾——第 47-50 行的注释说「政策存在而收据缺失 ⇒ 无法证明 venv 来源一致 ⇒ 拒跑」，而 `: > .venv/k3dge-source.txt`（或 init 被中断写出 0 字节文件、首行是空行值在第二行）与「删掉收据」在这里等价，却是放行。`Test-Path` 只证明 inode 存在，不证明内容有证据力。建议：空/不可用收据按缺失同等处理并 `exit 2`；`gate.py:76-77` 的 `if _rec and ...` 同形，需一并收紧以免三轨漂移。 | scripts/gate.ps1:56 | 已修 | 与 ocr2-007 同源（`scripts/gate.ps1:56`）：空/空白/BOM-only 收据拒跑；资产与仓内双份同改 | 双文件 `diff -q` 一致 | 未复核 |  |
| ocr2-014 | 2026-10-02 | 高 | P1 | 缺陷 | 两个问题，都会把失败的闸报成通过/失败： 1. **退出码可能是陈旧值 ⇒ 假通过**：`Get-Command k3dge` 命中的可能是 `.ps1`/`.cmd` 垫片或 function（第 70 行的注释正是这么说的），这类调用**不会刷新 `$LASTEXITCODE`**。此时 `$rc` 取到的是本脚本前面 `& python3 ...` 留下的值（政策来自 pyproject 时必然执行过，且成功=0），或者是 `$null`；`$rc = 0` 的初始化又把 `$null` 也洗成 0 → `exit 0`，闸在失败的判定核上返回 PASS。建议取码前判空，或在调用前设哨兵（`$global:LASTEXITCODE = 1` / 记录旧值以检测是否被刷新），或改用 `Start-Process -Wait -PassThru` 拿 `ExitCode`。 2. **`$k3dge.Source` 对 function/alias 为空 ⇒ 假失败**：`AliasInfo`/`FunctionInfo` 的 `Source` 是模块名（内建为空串/$null），`& $null` 抛 "Cannot bind argument to parameter 'Path'" 被 catch 成 `exit 127`，明明有可用的 `k3dge` 却报「无法执行」。建议按 `CommandType` 分派：`$target = if ($k3dge.Source) { $k3dge.Source } else { $k3dge.Definition }`，或对外部命令之外的类型直接拒绝并出声（因为那不是可校验的 exe）。 | scripts/gate.ps1:76 | 已修 | 非原生命令（function/alias）不刷新 `$LASTEXITCODE`：只对 `Application` 取码，其余用 `$?` 定 0/1；不再拿前面 `python3` 的陈旧 0 当通过 | 资产与 `scripts/gate.ps1` 双份同改 | 未复核 |  |
| ocr2-015 | 2026-10-02 | 高 | P1 | 缺陷 | Fail-open: an existing but blank receipt satisfies the policy. The guard `if _rec and ...` means `.venv/k3dge-source.txt` whose first line is empty/whitespace skips the comparison entirely and the gate proceeds — i.e. the exact "cannot prove the venv source" condition the block 10 lines above hard-fails on (missing file ⇒ exit 2). This is reachable in production: `scripts/init.ps1` writes `$srcRec + "`n"`, so an empty recorded source produces a file that exists but has an empty first line. The current tests only cover `unlink()` (missing file), never an empty/whitespace receipt, so a regression here stays green. Treat an empty receipt as unprovable and exit 2. | scripts/gate.py:76-77 | 已修 | gate.py 空/空白收据与"缺失"同等拒跑（exit 2）；资产与 `scripts/gate.py` 同改 | 双文件一致；空收据不再跳过比较 | 未复核 |  |
| ocr2-016 | 2026-10-02 | 高 | P1 | 缺陷 | Fail-open on the path that is supposed to fail closed: the mismatch check requires `[ -n "$_REC" ]`, so a receipt file that **exists but yields an empty first line** (empty file, leading blank line, BOM/CR-only, whitespace-only, or unreadable ⇒ `read` fails and `_REC=""`) silently passes the gate. That contradicts the branch above, which correctly treats a *missing* receipt as unprovable and exits 2 — `touch .venv/k3dge-source.txt` (or truncating it) is a cheaper bypass than deleting it. Note the line-based `IFS= read -r _REC` also only ever sees line 1, so an init/hand-written receipt with a leading blank line is indistinguishable from "no evidence". | scripts/gate.sh:47 | 已修 | gate.sh 空/空白收据拒跑（exit 2）；资产与 `scripts/gate.sh` 同改 | 双文件一致 | 未复核 |  |
| ocr2-017 | 2026-10-02 | 高 | P1 | 安全 | The source-integrity gate is only advisory on this branch: when a policy is declared (`_WANT` non-empty) but `.venv/bin/k3dge` is missing/lost its +x bit, the script emits a WARN and then execs an **unvalidated** binary from PATH, exiting with *its* status. So `rm .venv/bin/k3dge` (or a `chmod -x`, or a venv rebuild that lands a different `k3dge` in a pipx/global install) turns the whole receipt check into noise while still producing a green `check` result — the receipt that was just validated describes a venv that is not being executed. For a fail-closed governance gate, a declared policy plus an unusable local core should refuse rather than substitute: exit 2 here and tell the user to re-run init (only fall back to PATH when `_WANT` is empty, i.e. genuine legacy mode). | scripts/gate.sh:61-66 | 有意留 | 设计取舍（ocr-158 明示"至少出声"）：无 venv 时拒跑会断掉全局回落可用性；WARN 已让用户看见来源未经校验。改拒跑需另立 ADR 改契约 | `gate.sh:63-66` 注释即契约；真绕过需同时删 venv 并污染 PATH，WARN 可见 | 未复核 |  |
| ocr2-018 | 2026-10-02 | 高 | P1 | 缺陷 | **`trap { Pop-Location } EXIT` is not valid PowerShell and never restores the caller's location on the paths that matter.** 1. `trap` only accepts `BREAK` / `CONTINUE` as its trailing action — `EXIT` is not a trap trigger, so this line is at best a parse error for the whole script (`.ps1` is compiled up-front: one bad token = nothing runs, and since no test ever invokes `pwsh` on this file — `tests/unit/templates/test_hooks_reach_downstream.py` only greps for this literal string — CI stays green on a script that cannot even load). 2. Even taking the trap at face value, traps fire **only on a terminating error**. The success path (`Write-Host "[k3dge] Done..."`) and both `exit 1` sites leave the pushed location on the stack, so the comment's claim "Push/Pop + trap ⇒ 任何出口都还原" is false: exactly the leak described in issue 376 still happens whenever the script completes normally, which is the common case. Use `try/finally`, which does run on normal completion, on `exit`, and on exceptions: ```powershell Push-Location $Root try { # ... rest of the script ... } finally { Pop-Location } ``` (Secondary, same caller-side-effect class: line 1's `$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"` also mutates the *caller's* scope when this file is dot-sourced — the pattern the header comment is trying to avoid. | ocr2-092 | 2026-10-02 | 高 | P1 | 缺陷 | The claim on the previous line is not what the library does, and exit status alone is not evidence of a *fresh* attestation. `attest.append_to_message()` (src/k3dge/engine/attest.py:116) short-circuits with `return body` as soon as **any** line matches `LINE_RE = ^k3dge-commit: (.+?) @ (.+?) #([a-z]+)$` — a purely shape check, with no comparison against the current window/`git write-tree`. Forgery: any hand-written `k3dge-commit: someone @ 2020-01-01T00:00:00Z #zephyr` line satisfies `LINE_RE` and is therefore *preserved* instead of replaced (and with the `DEFAULT_SECRET` fallback the expected word is computable by anyone). So the "detect commits that bypassed the governed path" property is spoofable by the committer, who is exactly the actor this trailer is meant to constrain. The hook must not treat `commit-attest`'s exit code as proof. Either push the fix into the library (`append_to_message` recomputes whenever the existing line's token does not match the current `window` + staged tree) and expose it as an explicit flag, or have the hook re-verify after the rewrite (e.g. a `gate.py verify-message --file "$1"` that recomputes against the index tree and fails on mismatch/stale/forged lines). | ocr2-381 | 2026-10-02 | 中 | P2 | 缺陷 | The pattern is looser than the naming convention it claims to gate. `pure_schema.check_filename` compiles it with a plain `re.compile()` and applies it with `.match()`, with no `re.ASCII`, so: - `\d` and `\w` are Unicode classes: `２０２６-０９-２１-note.md` (fullwidth digits) or a slug in any non-ASCII script passes, contradicting the `YYYY-MM-DD` + ASCII kebab-case convention documented in `docs/memo/AUTHORING.md`. - `\d{2}` accepts impossible dates, so `2026-99-99-x.md` (or `2026-13-32-x.md`) clears the gate — the violation path silently succeeds. - In Python, `$` also matches just before a trailing newline, and `match()` only anchors the start, so a POSIX basename ending in `.md\n` (newlines are legal in filenames) is accepted. `\Z` is the correct end anchor. | ocr2-453 | 2026-10-02 | 中 | P2 | 正确性 | **The git fixtures inherit the caller's full environment, so they are neither hermetic nor safe.** - `GIT_DIR` / `GIT_WORK_TREE` / `GIT_CONFIG_*` exported in the developer's shell or a CI runner are *not* overridden by `cwd=repo`; git honours them ahead of cwd discovery. With those pointing at the real workspace, `git add` / `git commit` below would write into the **real repository** (and `git init` in `_make_repo` would fail or nest). This is the one place a "temp repo" test can touch production state. - Global/system config leaks in too: `core.excludesFile` matching `*.txt` makes `git add a.txt` abort, `commit.gpgsign=true` makes `git commit` abort, `core.hooksPath` / `init.templateDir` run the developer's hooks. The tests then break (or change behaviour) purely per-machine. - Diagnosability: `check=True` with `capture_output=True` surfaces only `Command '[...]' returned non-zero exit status 128.`; git's stderr lives on the exception object and is not printed, so a fixture failure hides the actual setup error. (Also note `TestResolveBase` re-copies the same init/identity/commit bootstrap instead of using `_make_repo`, whose docstring claims env isolation "only needs changing in one place" — the fix belongs in this helper so both call sites inherit it.) | tests/unit/engine/test_diff.py:30-32 | 待修 | 转：未建票 | 未核对 | 未复核 |  |
